# Harmonia OpenSpec — Expanded Subject and Capability Reconciliation

**Status:** Candidate classification and capability alignment for Solution Architect review. No governed Continuum changes authorised.

**Inputs:** Uploaded `openspec.zip` (`openspec/specs/**/spec.md`); supplied existing G01–G13 Subject hierarchy; local ArchiMate candidate `Harmonia-Enterprise-Continuum-1.1.18-001-003.xml` used **only for bounded extraction of existing Capability names and IDs**, not as architectural authority.

## Classification rules

- Retain G01–G13 unchanged. Propose G14–G18 below as **new candidate Subject groups**, not as established Continuum elements.
- Primary home represents the business-functional subject; secondary Subjects represent cross-cutting architectural concerns.
- A `UC-*` source produces one UseCase and one UserStory; an `SR-*` source produces a UserStory, preserving its distinct system requirement provenance.
- The 421 user-story texts are preserved verbatim from source fields. No invented requirement IDs or canonical IDs.
- Capability alignment is **provisional**: candidate CT3/Feature names and ArchiMate element identifiers are from the available candidate projection; semantic applicability, duplicates, canonical identity and containment must be confirmed in governed Continuum.
- Capability suggestions are **not** asserted Realization relationships or implementation commitments; where only a broad CT3 fit is available, this signals Feature-level gap/review.
- Source folders are evidence of domain scope, not authoritative Subject placement.

## Proposed Subject extensions

| Group | Name | Concern |
|---|---|---|
| **G14** | **Patient Identity and Demographics** | Business-functional motivation grouping for patient identity and demographics. |
| G14.01 | Identity Registration and Verification | Establishing and validating patient identity and supporting evidence. |
| G14.02 | Identity Matching and Reconciliation | Matching, linking, separating, merging and resolving patient identities. |
| G14.03 | Identity Maintenance and Status | Updating identity records, statuses and lifecycle. |
| G14.04 | Demographic and Contact Management | Maintaining patient demographic, contact and communication information. |
| G14.05 | Identity Access and Disclosure | Patient identity lookup, controlled disclosure and external identity enquiries. |
| G14.06 | Identity Quality and Oversight | Identity exceptions, risk, quality review and oversight. |
| **G15** | **Provider and Workforce Management** | Business-functional motivation grouping for provider and workforce management. |
| G15.01 | Provider Identity and Registration | Registering and maintaining provider and practitioner identity. |
| G15.02 | Credentials and Professional Standing | Qualifications, registration, credential verification and professional standing. |
| G15.03 | Affiliations, Roles and Practice Locations | Provider organisational relationships, roles, locations and service affiliations. |
| G15.04 | Provider Profile Maintenance | Maintaining professional profiles and provider contact details. |
| G15.05 | Provider Lifecycle and Administration | Provider onboarding, change, suspension and lifecycle administration. |
| **G16** | **Consent and Information Sharing** | Business-functional motivation grouping for consent and information sharing. |
| G16.01 | Consent Capture and Maintenance | Obtaining, recording, changing, renewing and withdrawing consent. |
| G16.02 | Consent Scope and Preferences | Managing consent purpose, duration, restrictions and sharing preferences. |
| G16.03 | Consent Verification and Decisions | Determining whether consent and relevant authority apply to an activity. |
| G16.04 | Representatives and Delegated Consent | Consent by representatives and verification of delegated authority. |
| G16.05 | Consent Communication and History | Presenting consent history, notices, discussions and change notifications. |
| G16.06 | Exceptional and Emergency Consent | Consent handling for emergency access and exceptional situations. |
| **G17** | **Terminology and Reference Data** | Business-functional motivation grouping for terminology and reference data. |
| G17.01 | Terminology Definition and Maintenance | Maintaining code systems, concepts, ValueSets and terminology definitions. |
| G17.02 | Terminology Mapping and Validation | Validating, comparing and mapping terminology and concepts. |
| G17.03 | Terminology Release and Versioning | Governed terminology publication, versioning and release lifecycle. |
| G17.04 | Reference Data Management | Maintaining governed reference values and reference datasets. |
| G17.05 | Reference Data Quality and Governance | Reference data ownership, verification, quality and stewardship. |
| **G18** | **Provider Directory** | Business-functional motivation grouping for provider directory. |
| G18.01 | Provider Directory Discovery | Searching and discovering providers, services and locations. |
| G18.02 | Directory Publication and Visibility | Publishing and controlling provider directory visibility. |
| G18.03 | Directory Information Maintenance | Updating and managing directory entries. |
| G18.04 | Directory Verification and Quality | Verifying and assessing directory information and changes. |
| G18.05 | Directory Synchronisation | Synchronising provider directory information with participating systems. |

## Reconciliation cautions

1. **Proposed Subject additions:** G14–G18 and their subgroup codes are recommendations, not previously authorised architectural elements. Review naming, overlaps and hierarchy before ADD.
2. **Capability model:** Matching is a first-pass semantic alignment to *existing named* CT3/Feature candidates. It is not evidence that each capability fully satisfies the source UseCase/UserStory. Review each candidate in the authoritative Continuum.
3. **Feature coverage:** CT3-only matches warrant examination for a more precise Feature; no new Feature has been invented.
4. **Identity and release scope:** Patient matching/EMPI-like use cases may represent future capabilities; classification does not authorise their implementation in R1/R2.
5. **SR provenance:** 58 `SR-*` sources provide UserStory candidates but must remain traceable to their separate system-requirement records; do not manufacture UseCases for them.
6. **Relationships:** Subject classification, Capability association, actor association and source provenance are distinct. Do not assume Composition, Realization or automatic duplication.
7. **Governance:** The Markdown RAD in `../../../../continuum` remains authoritative; the local ArchiMate XML is only a candidate projection and must not be silently treated as governed source.
